You are reviewing the work of a data agent that has just finished a task. The agent wrote code, ran it, and looked at results. You will see its complete trajectory, together with the task, the specification and documentation it used, and an index of all available data.

Your job is to find the places where the agent's work may be wrong, and to rank them by importance. Do not solve the task, do not write code, and do not judge whether the agent's final answer is correct as a whole.

Look for two kinds of problems:
- Places where the agent's work does not match what the task, specification, or documentation requires: scope and filters, metric definitions, which column a business term refers to, handling of special cases (missing values, zero denominators, invalid values), and the required form, granularity, and type of the output.
- Places where the agent's work may rest on a wrong understanding of the data: what a field or value means, what a row represents, whether a key is unique, whether records match in a join, what nulls mean, units, formats, where counting starts, and the time range or entities the data covers.

Listing a possible problem is not a claim that it is a real error. Many will turn out to be fine; they are listed so that they can be checked later. A possible problem that is unlikely but would change the result a lot if it were real should still be listed, ranked lower.

Two kinds of problems are easy to miss, so check for them explicitly:
- Omitted steps: processing that the specification or documentation requires but the code does not perform.
- Unused materials: files, tables, or columns in the index that bear on the task but that the agent did not use.

Rank problems by importance, combining two things: how likely it is to be a real problem given the materials you can see, and how much the agent's result would change if it is. A possible problem that would change little should rank low.

Return a JSON object whose keys are the names of every table or result the agent produced. For each, give an object with:
- "items": an ordered list of the 10 most important possible problems, most important first. Each item has:
  - "problem": one sentence describing what may be wrong.
  - "about": "requirement" or "data".
  - "evidence_in_work": a short quote from the agent's code or output that shows where the problem is.
  - "source": a short quote from the task, specification, documentation, or index that the problem is based on, or null.
  - "importance": an integer from 1 (low) to 5 (high).
  - "reason": one short sentence explaining the importance.
- "fewer_reason": if you listed fewer than 10 items, explain why; otherwise null.
List fewer than 10 only if you cannot find 10 possible problems for that table or result.

Examples of possible problems (from an unrelated online-shop task; they show the form of an item, not what to look for):
- {"problem": "If the amount column in payments is in cents rather than US dollars, revenue would be off by a factor of 100.", "about": "data", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(p.amount) AS revenue_usd", "source": null, "importance": 2, "reason": "The column name suggests dollars, so this is unlikely, but the effect would be large."}
- {"problem": "Revenue is computed from total_charged, which may include shipping fees, while the specification may define revenue as item price times quantity.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(o.total_charged) AS revenue_usd", "source": "revenue: sum of item_price * quantity", "importance": 4, "reason": "If the specification's definition applies, every row of revenue would be too high."}
- {"problem": "The specification requires converting created_at to UTC before deriving order_date, but the code takes the date directly.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "CAST(o.created_at AS DATE) AS order_date", "source": "convert created_at to UTC before deriving order_date", "importance": 5, "reason": "Orders near midnight would be assigned to the wrong day."}

=== Task ===
## Task Description:
You are a professional Data Engineer responsible for implementing a complete SQL data pipeline based on data contract documents.

## Objectives
Populate the SQL files in the `./sql/` directory according to the specifications in `docs/data_contract.yaml` and `config/layer_dependencies.yaml`. Your task is clear: write the necessary SQL code to ensure `run.py` executes successfully.

You MUST carefully review the content of `docs/data_contract.yaml`. You may use tools to view it. Since the file content is long, you can view it in chunks. Your task is to complete the SQL file writing based on the content of this file.

## Project Background
* Raw data has been loaded into `xxxx_start.duckdb` (Note: 'xxxx' is a placeholder for the project name, such as `greenhouse_start.duckdb` or `google_ads_start.duckdb`; 'xxxx' appearing below carries the same meaning).
* Currently, running `python run.py` completes the data transformation and outputs `xxxx.duckdb`.
* The existing SQL project is stored in the `sql/` folder.
* The current requirements documentation is located in `docs/data_contract.yaml`.

**Implementation Requirements:**
1. **Strictly Adhere to Configuration**:
    * All SQL logic and dependencies must be written strictly according to the `staging`, `intermediate`, and `marts` hierarchy defined in the YAML configuration files.

2. **File and Directory Structure**:
    * SQL files must be created in the corresponding level subdirectories (e.g., `sql/staging/`, `sql/intermediate/`, `sql/marts/`).
    * Strictly follow predefined file naming conventions and coding styles.

3. **Use Pure DuckDB Syntax**:
    * All SQL code must be compatible with DuckDB.
    * **Do NOT** end SQL statements with a semicolon (`;`) at the end of the file.

4. **No dbt Syntax**:
    * You must reference upstream tables directly using the `schema.table_name` format.
    * **Do NOT** use the dbt `ref()` function.

    **Examples**:
    * **Correct ✅**:
```sql
    with account_base as (
    select *
    from staging.stg_salesforce__account
    where not is_deleted
    ),

    account_enriched as (
    select
    account_id,
    ...
```

* **Incorrect ❌**:
```sql
    WITH accounts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__account') }}}}
    ),
    contacts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__contact') }}}}
    ),
    ...
```
Output Standard: Ensure the generated SQL files can be successfully executed by run.py to produce high-quality data results that meet business requirements.

## Data Contract Documentation (docs/data_contract.yaml):

```yaml
version: "1.0"
metadata:
  description: "Complete data pipeline specification for designated source systems"
  author: "Data Engineering Team"
  pipeline_phases: ["staging_contract", "modeling_spec"]
  domain: "Advertising Analytics"
  platforms:
    - "amazon_ads"
    - "google_ads"
    - "facebook_ads"
    - "microsoft_ads"
    - "linkedin_ads"
    - "apple_search_ads"
    - "pinterest_ads"
    - "reddit_ads"
    - "snapchat_ads"
    - "tiktok_ads"
    - "twitter_ads"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Phase 1: Define transformations from raw data to "clean" layered tables (staging) #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
staging_contract:
  purpose: "Transform raw data into clean, validated staging tables with consistent schema, data types and business logic"
  quality_actions:
    correct:
      description: "Automatically fix deterministic format issues such as email casing, whitespace trimming, and standardized date formats"
      examples:
        - "LOWER(TRIM(email))"
        - "standardize timezone formats"
        - "normalize phone numbers"
        # Rule 1: Platform name standardization
        - "UPPER(TRIM(platform)) for consistent platform naming"
        # Rule 2: Date format standardization
        - "DATE_FORMAT(date_day) = 'YYYY-MM-DD' for date consistency"
        # Rule 3: Negative clicks correction
        - "CASE WHEN clicks < 0 THEN 0 ELSE clicks END for negative value correction"
        # Rule 4: Click-through rate consistency
        - "click_through_rate = clicks / NULLIF(impressions, 0) when CTR doesn't match"
        # Rule 5: Cost decimal rounding
        - "ROUND(cost, 2) for currency precision standardization"

    delete_row:
      description: "Remove entire row when records contain severe data quality issues that make them unusable downstream."
      examples:
        - "Explicitly marked for deletion"
        - "Confirmed duplicates"
        - "Invalid primary keys"
        - "Essential business attributes contain irrecoverable errors"
        # Rule 6: Platform validation
        - "Delete rows with NULL platform values (primary identifier)"
        # Rule 7: Invalid ad_group_id pattern
        - "Delete rows where ad_group_id contains special characters or is empty"
        # Rule 8: Future date validation
        - "Delete rows with date_day > CURRENT_DATE (future data)"

    nullify_field:
      description: "Set invalid field values to NULL when the field is non-critical and record can still be useful"
      examples:
        - "malformed optional URLs"
        - "invalid secondary contact information"
        - "corrupted metadata fields"
        # Rule 9: Invalid bid strategy
        - "Set campaign_bidding_strategy to NULL when value is not in predefined enum list"
        # Rule 10: Click-through rate out of bounds
        - "Set click_through_rate to NULL when value < 0 OR value > 1.0"
      
  tables:
[Per-table sections of the data contract are omitted here. The sections relevant to each review appear under "Relevant specification and documentation".]
modeling_spec:
  purpose: "Transform staging data through intermediate models to final business-ready marts with enriched analytics"

  intermediate_models:
  marts_models:

```

Important: You can only interact with the provided environment. Do not ask a human for help.


When you believe the task is completed, you must call the "finish" tool to end the session. If you still need to develop or debug code, do not call "finish"; continue and complete everything first.

=== Specification and documentation ===
## stg_amazon_ads__ad_group_report

    - name: "stg_amazon_ads__ad_group_report"
      description: "Staging table for Amazon Ads  Ad Group Report"
      columns:
        - name: "platform"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "UPPER(platform) IN ('AMAZON_ADS','GOOGLE_ADS','FACEBOOK_ADS','MICROSOFT_ADS','LINKEDIN_ADS','APPLE_SEARCH_ADS','PINTEREST_ADS','REDDIT_ADS','SNAPCHAT_ADS','TIKTOK_ADS','TWITTER_ADS')"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: "ad_group_id"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "ad_group_id IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(ad_group_id) != ''"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "REGEXP_LIKE(ad_group_id, '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$')"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: "date_day"
          data_type: DATE
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "date_day IS NULL OR (date_day >= '2020-01-01' AND date_day <= CURRENT_DATE)"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "date_day IS NULL OR date_day NOT IN ('1970-01-01','1900-01-01','9999-12-31')"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: "campaign_bidding_strategy"
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: "clicks"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "cost"
          data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        - name: "impressions"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "click_through_rate"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "cost_per_click"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "_dbt_loaded_at"
          data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "_dbt_loaded_at IS NOT NULL AND _dbt_loaded_at <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"
              on_failure: delete_row

## stg_amazon_ads__keyword_report

    - name: "stg_amazon_ads__keyword_report"
      description: "Staging table for Amazon Ads  Keyword Report"
      columns:
        - name: "platform"
          data_type: VARCHAR

              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "date_day IS NULL OR date_day NOT IN ('1970-01-01','1900-01-01','9999-12-31')"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: "campaign_id"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "campaign_id IS NULL OR (TRIM(campaign_id) != '' AND LENGTH(campaign_id) <= 50 AND REGEXP_LIKE(campaign_id, '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$'))"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: "ad_group_id"
          data_type: VARCHAR

          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: "budget_type"

        - name: "attributed_conversions_30_d"
          data_type: INTEGER

          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "cost_per_click"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "return_on_ad_spend"

## stg_apple_search_ads__ad_group_report

    - name: "stg_apple_search_ads__ad_group_report"
      description: "Staging table for Apple Search Ads  Ad Group Report"
      columns:
        - name: "platform"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "UPPER(platform) = 'APPLE_SEARCH_ADS'"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: "ad_group_id"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "ad_group_id IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(ad_group_id) != '' AND LENGTH(ad_group_id) <= 50"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "REGEXP_LIKE(ad_group_id, '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$')"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: "date_day"
          data_type: DATE
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "date_day IS NOT NULL AND date_day >= '2020-01-01' AND date_day <= CURRENT_DATE"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "date_day NOT IN ('1970-01-01','1900-01-01','9999-12-31')"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: "impressions"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "taps"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "tap_installs"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "conversions"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "new_downloads"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "redownloads"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "tap_new_downloads"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "tap_redownloads"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "spend"
          data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "spend >= 0 AND spend <= 999999999999.99"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "spend = ROUND(spend, 2)"
              on_failure: correct
            - rule: "spend IS NOT NULL OR spend = 0"
              on_failure: correct
        - name: "spend_currency"
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: "avg_cost_per_tap"
          data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        - name: "avg_cpt_currency"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "avg_cpt_currency IS NULL OR avg_cpt_currency IN ('USD','EUR','GBP','JPY','AUD','CAD')"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "LENGTH(avg_cpt_currency) <= 3"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "avg_cpt_currency = UPPER(TRIM(avg_cpt_currency))"
              on_failure: correct
        - name: "avg_cost_per_acquisition"
          data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        - name: "avg_cpa_currency"
          data_type: VARCHAR
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "avg_cpa_currency IS NULL OR avg_cpa_currency IN ('USD','EUR','GBP','JPY','AUD','CAD')"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "LENGTH(avg_cpa_currency) <= 3"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "avg_cpa_currency = UPPER(TRIM(avg_cpa_currency))"
              on_failure: correct
        - name: "tap_through_rate"
          data_type: DECIMAL(10,6)
        - name: "conversion_rate"
          data_type: DECIMAL(10,6)
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "conversion_rate BETWEEN 0 AND 1"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: "lat_on_installs"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "lat_off_installs"
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: "calculated_tap_through_rate"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "cost_per_tap"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "cost_per_conversion"
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: "_dbt_loaded_at"
          data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "_dbt_loaded_at <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"
              on_failure: delete_row

## int_ad_reporting__ad_group_report

    - name: int_ad_reporting__ad_group_report
      description: Standardized Amazon Ads ad group report with derived metrics and placeholders for unsupported fields
      grain: One row per source record from staging.stg_amazon_ads__ad_group_report (duplicates preserved)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__ad_group_report
      business_logic: '- Read all rows from staging.stg_amazon_ads__ad_group_report; no joins or aggregations are performed; no ordering is applied. - Set platform to the constant string "amazon_ads". - Keys and references: - ad_group_id is converted to text (VARCHAR) from the source value; date_day is passed through; account_id and campaign_id are set to NULL. - Attributes unsupported by Amazon Ads in ad group reports are set to NULL: ad_network_type, device. - Performance metrics: - impressions and clicks default to 0 when missing; interactions is a constant 0. - Cost: - cost defaults to 0 when missing. - Conversions and view-through: - conversions, conversions_value, view_through_conversions are constants 0 (not provided at ad group level). - Viewability metrics: - active_view_impressions, active_view_measurable_impressions, active_view_measurable_cost, active_view_measurability, active_view_viewability are set to NULL. - Amazon-attribution metrics: - attributed_sales_14_d, attributed_sales_30_d, attributed_conversions_14_d, attributed_conversions_30_d are constants 0. - Derived metrics are computed from the original source values (before defaults to 0 are applied) with explicit rounding: - click_through_rate: when source impressions > 0, compute (source clicks ÷ source impressions) × 100 and round to 4 decimal places; otherwise 0; if source clicks is NULL in the computation branch, the result is NULL. - cost_per_click: when source clicks > 0, compute source cost ÷ source clicks and round to 2 decimal places; otherwise 0; if source cost is NULL in the computation branch, the result is NULL. - cost_per_thousand_impressions: when source impressions > 0, compute (source cost ÷ source impressions) × 1000 and round to 2 decimal places; otherwise 0; if source cost is NULL in the computation branch, the result is NULL. - cost_per_conversion: always 0 (no conversions). - return_on_ad_spend: always 0 (no revenue/conversions). - interaction_event_types is set to NULL; _dbt_loaded_at is passed through from the source.'
      columns:
      - name: platform
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant string 'amazon_ads' identifying the source platform
      - name: account_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Account identifier; always NULL in this model
      - name: ad_group_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Ad group identifier stored as text from the source without trimming or case changes
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Report date passed through from source
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Campaign identifier; always NULL in this model
      - name: ad_network_type
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Ad network type; not applicable for Amazon Ads and set to NULL
      - name: device
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Device segmentation; not applicable for Amazon Ads and set to NULL
      - name: impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Impression count with NULLs treated as 0
      - name: clicks
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Click count with NULLs treated as 0
      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Interaction count; constant 0 as Amazon Ads does not track interactions here
      - name: cost
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Ad spend with NULLs treated as 0
      - name: conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Conversion count; constant 0 (not available at ad group level)
      - name: conversions_value
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Conversion value; constant 0 (not available at ad group level)
      - name: view_through_conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: View-through conversions; constant 0 (not tracked)
      - name: active_view_impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Active View impressions; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: active_view_measurable_impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Active View measurable impressions; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: active_view_measurable_cost
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Active View measurable cost; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: active_view_measurability
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Active View measurability; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: active_view_viewability
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Active View viewability; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: attributed_sales_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Attributed sales within 14 days; constant 0
      - name: attributed_sales_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Attributed sales within 30 days; constant 0
      - name: attributed_conversions_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Attributed conversions within 14 days; constant 0
      - name: attributed_conversions_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Attributed conversions within 30 days; constant 0
      - name: click_through_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CTR percent; when source impressions > 0 compute clicks ÷ impressions × 100 rounded to 4 decimals, else 0; NULL if clicks is NULL in the computation branch
      - name: cost_per_click
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPC; when source clicks > 0 compute cost ÷ clicks rounded to 2 decimals, else 0; NULL if cost is NULL in the computation branch
      - name: cost_per_thousand_impressions
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPM; when source impressions > 0 compute cost ÷ impressions × 1000 rounded to 2 decimals, else 0; NULL if cost is NULL in the computation branch
      - name: cost_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost per conversion; constant 0 due to no conversions
      - name: return_on_ad_spend
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: ROAS; constant 0 due to no revenue/conversions
      - name: interaction_event_types
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Interaction event types; set to NULL (not available)
      - name: _dbt_loaded_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: ETL load timestamp passed through from source

    - name: int_ad_reporting__ad_report
      description: Unified ad-level report standardizing Facebook Ads and Apple Search Ads by appending source rows

      source_models:

      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER

      - name: cost

## int_ad_reporting__campaign_report

    - name: int_ad_reporting__campaign_report
      description: Unified campaign-level metrics for ad reporting (currently Amazon Ads only)
      grain: One row per source row in staging.stg_amazon_ads__campaign_report; duplicates preserved
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__campaign_report
      business_logic: '- Read all rows from staging.stg_amazon_ads__campaign_report; no joins, filters, or aggregations; preserve source duplicates. - Set platform to the constant "amazon_ads". - Identifiers and attributes: - campaign_id: convert to string; date_day: passthrough. - account_id, campaign_name, ad_network_type, device: set to NULL (not provided by Amazon Ads campaign report). - Standardize metrics: - Replace NULL with 0 for impressions, clicks, cost, conversions (mapped from 30-day attributed purchases), conversions_value (mapped from 30-day attributed sales), attributed_sales_14_d (mapped from 7-day attributed sales), attributed_sales_30_d (mapped from 30-day attributed sales), attributed_conversions_14_d (mapped from 30-day attributed purchases), and attributed_conversions_30_d (mapped from 30-day attributed purchases). - interactions and view_through_conversions are set to 0. - Active View metrics (impressions, measurable_impressions, measurable_cost, measurability, viewability) are set to NULL. - Calculated metrics (rounding and denominator checks): - All calculations use the original source values for inputs; if the denominator is NULL or not greater than 0, return 0; otherwise perform the division and round as specified. If the numerator is NULL when computing, the result is NULL. - click_through_rate: clicks divided by impressions times 100, rounded to 4 decimals. - cost_per_click: cost divided by clicks, rounded to 2 decimals. - cost_per_thousand_impressions: cost divided by impressions times 1000, rounded to 2 decimals. - cost_per_conversion: cost divided by 30-day attributed purchases, rounded to 2 decimals. - return_on_ad_spend: 30-day attributed sales value divided by cost, rounded to 4 decimals; returns 0 when cost or sales value is not greater than 0 or is NULL. - interaction_event_types is set to NULL; _dbt_loaded_at is a direct passthrough from the source.'
      columns:
      - name: platform
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant platform identifier 'amazon_ads'.
      - name: account_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (not available in Amazon Ads campaign report).
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Campaign identifier converted to string from source.
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Reporting date from source.
      - name: campaign_name
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (not provided by Amazon Ads campaign report).
      - name: ad_network_type
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (concept not present in Amazon Ads campaign report).
      - name: device
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (no device segmentation in Amazon Ads campaign report).
      - name: impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Impression count with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: clicks
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Click count with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to 0 (Amazon Ads does not track interactions).
      - name: cost
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Ad spend with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 30-day attributed conversions with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: conversions_value
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 30-day attributed sales value with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: view_through_conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to 0 (not tracked by Amazon Ads).
      - name: active_view_impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (metric not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: active_view_measurable_impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (metric not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: active_view_measurable_cost
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (metric not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: active_view_measurability
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (metric not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: active_view_viewability
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (metric not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: attributed_sales_14_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Mapped from 7-day attributed sales with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: attributed_sales_30_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Mapped from 30-day attributed sales with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: attributed_conversions_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Mapped from 30-day attributed conversions with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: attributed_conversions_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Mapped from 30-day attributed conversions with NULL replaced by 0.
      - name: click_through_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Clicks divided by impressions times 100, rounded to 4 decimals; returns 0 when impressions <= 0 or NULL; returns NULL if clicks is NULL when impressions > 0.
      - name: cost_per_click
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost divided by clicks, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when clicks <= 0 or NULL; returns NULL if cost is NULL when clicks > 0.
      - name: cost_per_thousand_impressions
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost divided by impressions times 1000, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when impressions <= 0 or NULL; returns NULL if cost is NULL when impressions > 0.
      - name: cost_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost divided by 30-day attributed conversions, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when conversions <= 0 or NULL; returns NULL if cost is NULL when conversions > 0.
      - name: return_on_ad_spend
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sales value divided by cost, rounded to 4 decimals; returns 0 when cost or sales value is not greater than 0 or is NULL.
      - name: interaction_event_types
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Set to NULL (not available for Amazon Ads).
      - name: _dbt_loaded_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Load timestamp from source.

## int_ad_reporting__keyword_report

    - name: int_ad_reporting__keyword_report
      description: Intermediate keyword-level unified report (Amazon Ads only) with standardized fields and derived CTR/CPC/CPM/CPA/ROAS
      grain: One row per source record (keyword_id, date_day, campaign_id, ad_group_id) from staging.stg_amazon_ads__keyword_report; duplicates preserved
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__keyword_report
      business_logic: '- Rows are passed through from staging.stg_amazon_ads__keyword_report; no joins, filters, de-duplication, or ordering. - Set platform to "amazon_ads". - Cast keyword_id to VARCHAR; pass through date_day, campaign_id, ad_group_id, keyword_type, match_type; set account_id = NULL and keyword_text = NULL. - Metrics null handling: - impressions and clicks: replace NULL with 0; interactions: constant 0. - cost: replace NULL with 0 (2-decimal currency). - conversions: use attributed_conversions_30_d with NULL replaced by 0. - conversions_value: use attributed_sales_30_d with NULL replaced by 0 (2-decimal currency). - view_through_conversions: constant 0. - attributed_sales_14_d/30_d and attributed_conversions_14_d/30_d: replace NULL with 0. - quality_score, first_page_cpc, top_of_page_cpc, first_position_cpc, search_impression_share, search_rank_lost_impression_share, search_budget_lost_impression_share: always NULL. - Derived metrics are calculated from raw source values before zero-filling: - click_through_rate: if impressions > 0, compute (clicks ÷ impressions) × 100 and round to 4 decimals; otherwise 0; returns NULL when impressions > 0 and clicks is NULL. - cost_per_click: if clicks > 0, compute cost ÷ clicks and round to 2 decimals; otherwise 0; returns NULL when clicks > 0 and cost is NULL. - cost_per_thousand_impressions: if impressions > 0, compute (cost ÷ impressions) × 1,000 and round to 2 decimals; otherwise 0; returns NULL when impressions > 0 and cost is NULL. - cost_per_conversion: if attributed_conversions_30_d > 0, compute cost ÷ conversions and round to 2 decimals; otherwise 0; returns NULL when conversions > 0 and cost is NULL. - return_on_ad_spend: if cost > 0 and attributed_sales_30_d > 0, compute sales ÷ cost and round to 4 decimals; otherwise 0; never NULL. - _dbt_loaded_at is passed through unchanged.'

        data_type: VARCHAR

        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Keyword text; always NULL in current Amazon Ads source
      - name: impressions

        description: Impressions; NULLs become 0; never NULL
      - name: clicks
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Clicks; NULLs become 0; never NULL
      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Total interactions; constant 0 for Amazon Ads
      - name: cost

## int_ad_reporting__search_report

    - name: int_ad_reporting__search_report
      description: Intermediate Amazon Ads search term performance model with standardized metrics and derived rates
      grain: One row per input record from staging.stg_amazon_ads__search_report (per search_term × date_day × campaign_id × ad_group_id × keyword_id)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__search_report
      business_logic: '- Source rows are passed through without filtering or aggregation; one output row per source row. - Set platform to the constant value ''amazon_ads''. - Carry over identifiers: search_term (cast to text), date_day, campaign_id, ad_group_id, keyword_id; set account_id to null. - Carry over attributes: match_type and query; set search_term_match_type to null. - Replace nulls with zero for numeric metrics: impressions, clicks, cost, attributed_sales_14_d, attributed_sales_30_d, attributed_conversions_14_d, attributed_conversions_30_d. - Set interactions and view_through_conversions to 0. - Map conversions to 30-day attributed conversions and conversions_value to 30-day attributed sales (both after nulls are replaced with zero). - Text features: search_term_length is the number of characters in search_term; search_term_word_count is the number of space-delimited words; both are null if search_term is null. - Set relevance and coverage metrics to null: quality_score, search_impression_share, search_exact_match_impression_share, search_rank_lost_impression_share, search_budget_lost_impression_share. - Derived metrics (computed after nulls are replaced with zero): - click_through_rate: clicks divided by impressions, multiplied by 100, rounded to 4 decimals; returns 0 when impressions is 0. - cost_per_click: cost divided by clicks, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when clicks is 0. - cost_per_thousand_impressions: cost divided by impressions, multiplied by 1,000, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when impressions is 0. - cost_per_conversion: cost divided by 30-day attributed conversions, rounded to 2 decimals; returns 0 when conversions is 0. - return_on_ad_spend: 30-day attributed sales divided by cost, rounded to 4 decimals; returns 0 when either cost or sales is 0. - Pass through _dbt_loaded_at.'
      columns:

        description: Ad group identifier passed through from source

        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Keyword identifier passed through from source
      - name: match_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Keyword match type passed through from source
      - name: query
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Reported query string passed through from source
      - name: search_term_match_type
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Search term match type not provided and always null
      - name: impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Impression count with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: clicks
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Click count with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Interaction count set to 0
      - name: cost
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Ad cost with nulls replaced by 0 and stored at two-decimal precision
      - name: conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 30-day attributed conversions with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: conversions_value
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 30-day attributed sales with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: view_through_conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: View-through conversions set to 0
      - name: attributed_sales_14_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 14-day attributed sales with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: attributed_sales_30_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 30-day attributed sales with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: attributed_conversions_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 14-day attributed conversions with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: attributed_conversions_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 30-day attributed conversions with nulls replaced by 0
      - name: search_term_length
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of characters in search_term; null when search_term is null
      - name: search_term_word_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of space-delimited words in search_term; null when search_term is null
      - name: quality_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Quality score not provided and always null
      - name: search_impression_share
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Search impression share not provided and always null
      - name: search_exact_match_impression_share
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Exact match impression share not provided and always null
      - name: search_rank_lost_impression_share
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Rank lost impression share not provided and always null
      - name: search_budget_lost_impression_share
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Budget lost impression share not provided and always null
      - name: click_through_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Percentage of impressions that resulted in clicks; rounded to 4 decimals; 0 when impressions is 0; computed after replacing nulls with zero
      - name: cost_per_click
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average cost per click; rounded to 2 decimals; 0 when clicks is 0; computed after replacing nulls with zero
      - name: cost_per_thousand_impressions
        data_type: DOUBLE

      - name: cost_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE

      - name: return_on_ad_spend

      - name: _dbt_loaded_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE

## int_ad_reporting__unified_campaign_metrics

    - name: int_ad_reporting__unified_campaign_metrics
      description: Unified daily campaign metrics across platforms with standardized KPIs, performance tiers, rolling 365‑day filter, and data quality screening
      grain: One row per platform, campaign_id, date_day
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__campaign_report
      - staging.stg_google_ads__campaign_report
      - staging.stg_facebook_ads__ad_report
      - raw.linkedin_ad_analytics_by_campaign_data
      business_logic: 'Combine four platform feeds into a standardized base and apply filters per source: - Amazon Ads (staging.stg_amazon_ads__campaign_report): - Map fields: platform=''amazon_ads''; campaign_type from campaign_bidding_strategy; daily_budget from campaign_budget_amount; currency from campaign_budget_currency_code; cost from source; revenue_7d from sales_7_d; revenue_30d from sales_30_d; conversions from purchases_30_d; conversion_type=''PRODUCT_SALES''; CTR, CPC, ROAS from source; source_updated_at from _dbt_loaded_at. - Keep rows where clicks, impressions, and cost are all non‑negative. - Google Ads (staging.stg_google_ads__campaign_report): - Map fields: platform=''google_ads''; campaign_type from ad_network_type with default ''SEARCH''; daily_budget NULL; currency=''USD''; cost from spend; revenue_7d and revenue_30d both from conversions_value; conversions from conversions; conversion_type=''ONLINE_CONVERSION''. - KPIs: CTR as percentage (clicks ÷ impressions), rounded to 4 decimals and set to 0 when impressions=0; CPC as cost ÷ clicks, rounded to 2 decimals and set to 0 when clicks=0; ROAS as revenue_7d ÷ cost when both are positive, else 0. - Set source_updated_at to the execution timestamp; keep rows where clicks, impressions, and spend are all non‑negative. - Facebook Ads (staging.stg_facebook_ads__ad_report): - Map fields: platform=''facebook_ads''; campaign_id from ad_id; campaign_type=''FACEBOOK_FEED''; daily_budget NULL; currency=''USD''; clicks from inline_link_clicks defaulting to 0; impressions defaulting to 0; cost from spend defaulting to 0; revenue_7d=0; revenue_30d=0; conversions=0; conversion_type=''ENGAGEMENT''. - KPIs: CTR as percentage (clicks ÷ impressions), rounded to 4 decimals and set to 0 when impressions=0; CPC as cost ÷ clicks, rounded to 2 decimals and set to 0 when clicks=0; ROAS=0. - Set source_updated_at to the execution timestamp; keep rows where spend and impressions are non‑negative. - LinkedIn Ads (raw.linkedin_ad_analytics_by_campaign_data): - Map fields: platform=''linkedin_ads''; date_day from day; campaign_type=''LINKEDIN_SPONSORED''; daily_budget NULL; currency=''USD''; clicks defaulting to 0; impressions cast to integer and defaulting to 0; cost cast to decimal and defaulting to 0; revenue_7d=0; revenue_30d=0; conversions from conversion_value_in_local_currency cast to integer and defaulting to 0; conversion_type=''LEAD_GENERATION''. - KPIs: CTR as percentage (clicks ÷ impressions), rounded to 4 decimals and set to 0 when impressions=0; CPC as cost ÷ clicks, rounded to 2 decimals and set to 0 unless both clicks and cost are positive; ROAS=0. - Set source_updated_at to the execution timestamp; keep rows where cost_in_usd is non‑negative. Enhance with performance metrics (computed on the full base before final filters): - ctr_performance_tier: platform‑specific thresholds on CTR (percent): - amazon_ads: HIGH when ≥0.5; MEDIUM when ≥0.2; else LOW. - google_ads: HIGH when ≥2.0; MEDIUM when ≥0.8; else LOW. - facebook_ads: HIGH when ≥1.5; MEDIUM when ≥0.9; else LOW. - others: HIGH when ≥1.0; MEDIUM when ≥0.5; else LOW. - cost_efficiency_tier: VERY_EFFICIENT when CPC≤0.5; EFFICIENT when ≤1.5; MODERATE when ≤3.0; EXPENSIVE when ≤5.0; else VERY_EXPENSIVE. - roas_performance_tier: for amazon_ads and google_ads, classify ROAS as EXCELLENT when ≥4.0; GOOD when ≥2.0; BREAK_EVEN when ≥1.0; LOSS_MAKING when >0; else NO_REVENUE; other platforms NOT_APPLICABLE. - campaign_age_days: days since the earliest observed date_day for the platform and campaign_id in the full base (not limited to the 365‑day window). - volume_tier: HIGH_VOLUME when impressions≥10,000; MEDIUM_VOLUME when ≥1,000; LOW_VOLUME when ≥100; else MINIMAL_VOLUME. Final selection and additional computations: - cost_per_mille (CPM): cost per 1,000 impressions, rounded to 2 decimals; set to 0 when impressions=0. - cost_per_conversion: cost ÷ conversions, rounded to 2 decimals; set to 0 when conversions=0. - lifecycle_stage from campaign_age_days: LAUNCH when ≤7; OPTIMIZATION when ≤30; MATURE when ≤90; else ESTABLISHED. - has_data_quality_issues: TRUE when clicks>impressions or cost<0 or CTR>100 or CPC>50; otherwise FALSE. - processed_at set to the execution timestamp. Apply final filters: - Keep rows where date_day is within the last 365 days relative to execution. - Keep rows where impressions>0. - Exclude rows flagged with has_data_quality_issues (output rows have has_data_quality_issues=FALSE). Notes: - Inputs are combined without deduplication; no ordering is enforced. - Rounding: CTR to 4 decimals (for computed platforms), CPC to 2 decimals, CPM to 2 decimals, cost_per_conversion to 2 decimals. - Units: CTR is a percentage; CPC/CPM/cost_per_conversion and cost are in the stated currency (USD for Google/Facebook/LinkedIn; Amazon uses its source currency); ROAS is a ratio. - CURRENT_DATE and CURRENT_TIMESTAMP are evaluated at query execution time.'
      columns:
      - name: platform
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Ad platform identifier: ''amazon_ads'', ''google_ads'', ''facebook_ads'', or ''linkedin_ads'''
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source campaign identifier (Facebook uses ad_id as campaign_id)
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Metric date; only rows within the rolling last 365 days relative to execution are kept
      - name: campaign_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Platform-specific campaign type (Amazon=bidding strategy, Google=ad_network_type default 'SEARCH', Facebook='FACEBOOK_FEED', LinkedIn='LINKEDIN_SPONSORED')
      - name: daily_budget
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Daily budget amount (Amazon only from campaign_budget_amount; NULL for other platforms; currency given by currency column)
      - name: currency
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: ISO currency code for monetary fields (USD for Google/Facebook/LinkedIn; Amazon from campaign_budget_currency_code)
      - name: conversion_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Conversion attribution type by platform: ''PRODUCT_SALES'', ''ONLINE_CONVERSION'', ''ENGAGEMENT'', or ''LEAD_GENERATION'''
      - name: impressions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of impressions (non-negative in base; cast to integer on LinkedIn); final output enforces impressions>0
      - name: clicks
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of clicks (defaults to 0 where applicable); must be ≤ impressions in final output due to data quality filtering
      - name: cost
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Spend amount (Amazon in source currency; Google/Facebook/LinkedIn in USD); non-negative in base and non-negative in final output
      - name: revenue_7d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 7-day revenue proxy (Amazon=sales_7_d, Google=conversions_value, others=0)
      - name: revenue_30d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: 30-day revenue proxy (Amazon=sales_30_d, Google=conversions_value, others=0)
      - name: conversions
        data_type: DECIMAL(12,2)
        description: Conversion count/value (Amazon=purchases_30_d, Google=conversions, Facebook=0, LinkedIn from conversion_value_in_local_currency as integer)
      - name: click_through_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CTR as a percentage in [0,100] (Amazon from source; others as clicks ÷ impressions expressed as percent, rounded to 4 decimals; rows with CTR>100 are excluded)
      - name: cost_per_click
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPC (Amazon from source; others as cost ÷ clicks rounded to 2 decimals with 0 when clicks≤0); rows with CPC>50 are excluded
      - name: return_on_ad_spend
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: ROAS ratio (Amazon from source; Google as revenue_7d ÷ cost with 0 when inputs are not positive; others=0)
      - name: cost_per_mille
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPM as cost per 1,000 impressions rounded to 2 decimals; denominator is always >0 due to final filter
      - name: cost_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost per conversion as cost ÷ conversions rounded to 2 decimals when conversions>0 else 0
      - name: ctr_performance_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: CTR performance classification by platform (HIGH, MEDIUM, LOW) using platform-specific percent thresholds
      - name: cost_efficiency_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'CPC-based cost efficiency classification: VERY_EFFICIENT, EFFICIENT, MODERATE, EXPENSIVE, VERY_EXPENSIVE'
      - name: roas_performance_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: ROAS-based classification for Amazon/Google (EXCELLENT, GOOD, BREAK_EVEN, LOSS_MAKING, NO_REVENUE) else NOT_APPLICABLE
      - name: volume_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Impression volume classification: HIGH_VOLUME (≥10,000), MEDIUM_VOLUME (≥1,000), LOW_VOLUME (≥100), else MINIMAL_VOLUME'
      - name: campaign_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days since the earliest observed date_day for the platform+campaign_id in the base (pre 365‑day filter)
      - name: lifecycle_stage
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Stage by campaign_age_days: LAUNCH (≤7), OPTIMIZATION (≤30), MATURE (≤90), else ESTABLISHED'
      - name: has_data_quality_issues
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: TRUE if clicks>impressions or cost<0 or CTR>100 or CPC>50; final output only contains FALSE due to filtering
      - name: source_updated_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Source freshness timestamp (Amazon=_dbt_loaded_at; Google/Facebook/LinkedIn set to the execution timestamp)
      - name: processed_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Model processing timestamp set to the execution timestamp


## int_ad_reporting__url_report

    - name: int_ad_reporting__url_report
      description: Unified URL/Product-level ad performance derived from Amazon Ads product report with standardized metrics and platform tagging
      grain: One row per source row in staging.stg_amazon_ads__product_report
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_amazon_ads__product_report
      business_logic: '- Read all rows from staging.stg_amazon_ads__product_report with no aggregation, filtering, or de-duplication. - Set platform to the constant ''amazon_ads''. - Identifiers: url_or_product_id is advertised_asin as a string; date_day is copied; campaign_id and ad_group_id are copied; account_id and ad_id are set to NULL. - Product attributes: product_sku is advertised_sku; product_asin is advertised_asin; landing_page_url, final_url, and display_url are NULL; url_type is the constant ''product''. - Performance metrics default to 0 when null: impressions, clicks; interactions is set to 0. - Cost defaults to 0 when null. - Conversions: conversions equals attributed_conversions_30_d (defaults to 0 when null); conversions_value equals attributed_sales_30_d (defaults to 0 when null); view_through_conversions is 0. - Amazon-specific metrics default to 0 when null: attributed_sales_14_d/30_d, attributed_conversions_14_d/30_d, attributed_units_ordered_14_d/30_d. - URL engagement metrics (bounce_rate, pages_per_session, avg_session_duration, landing_page_experience, mobile_friendly_click_rate) are NULL. - Calculated metrics: - click_through_rate: (clicks ÷ impressions) × 100, rounded to 4 decimals; if impressions = 0 then 0. - cost_per_click: cost ÷ clicks, rounded to 2 decimals; if clicks = 0 then 0. - cost_per_thousand_impressions: (cost ÷ impressions) × 1000, rounded to 2 decimals; if impressions = 0 then 0. - cost_per_conversion: cost ÷ attributed_conversions_30_d, rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_conversions_30_d = 0 then 0. - return_on_ad_spend: attributed_sales_30_d ÷ cost, rounded to 4 decimals; if cost = 0 or attributed_sales_30_d = 0 then 0. - units_per_conversion: attributed_units_ordered_30_d ÷ attributed_conversions_30_d, rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_conversions_30_d = 0 then 0. - avg_order_value: attributed_sales_30_d ÷ attributed_units_ordered_30_d, rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_units_ordered_30_d = 0 then 0. - Carry through _dbt_loaded_at from the source unchanged.'
      columns:
      - name: platform
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant platform identifier set to 'amazon_ads'; non-null for all rows
      - name: url_or_product_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Product identifier from advertised_asin as a string; may be NULL if source ASIN is NULL
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Reporting date copied from source
      - name: account_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Account identifier not present in Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: campaign_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source campaign identifier copied without transformation
      - name: ad_group_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source ad group identifier copied without transformation
      - name: ad_id
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Ad identifier not present in Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: product_sku
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Advertised SKU copied from source
      - name: product_asin
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Advertised ASIN copied from source
      - name: landing_page_url
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Landing page URL not available for Amazon product report; always NULL (integer type retained for schema compatibility)
      - name: final_url
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Final URL not available for Amazon product report; always NULL (integer type retained for schema compatibility)
      - name: display_url
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Display URL not available for Amazon product report; always NULL (integer type retained for schema compatibility)
      - name: url_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant 'product' indicating product-level reporting
      - name: impressions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Impressions default to 0 when null; non-negative integer
      - name: clicks
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Clicks default to 0 when null; non-negative integer
      - name: interactions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Interactions set to constant 0; not derived from source
      - name: cost
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Ad cost defaults to 0 when null; two-decimal precision
      - name: conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Total conversions equal to attributed_conversions_30_d, defaulting to 0 when null
      - name: conversions_value
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Conversion value equals attributed_sales_30_d, defaulting to 0 when null
      - name: view_through_conversions
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: View-through conversions set to constant 0
      - name: attributed_sales_14_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Amazon attributed sales within 14 days default to 0 when null
      - name: attributed_sales_30_d
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Amazon attributed sales within 30 days default to 0 when null
      - name: attributed_conversions_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Amazon attributed conversions within 14 days default to 0 when null
      - name: attributed_conversions_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Amazon attributed conversions within 30 days default to 0 when null
      - name: attributed_units_ordered_14_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Amazon attributed units ordered within 14 days default to 0 when null
      - name: attributed_units_ordered_30_d
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Amazon attributed units ordered within 30 days default to 0 when null
      - name: bounce_rate
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Bounce rate not available for Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: pages_per_session
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Pages per session not available for Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: avg_session_duration
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Average session duration not available for Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: landing_page_experience
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Landing page experience score not available for Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: mobile_friendly_click_rate
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Mobile-friendly click rate not available for Amazon product report; always NULL
      - name: click_through_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CTR equals (clicks ÷ impressions) × 100 rounded to 4 decimals; if impressions=0 then 0
      - name: cost_per_click
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPC equals cost ÷ clicks rounded to 2 decimals; if clicks=0 then 0
      - name: cost_per_thousand_impressions
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: CPM equals (cost ÷ impressions) × 1000 rounded to 2 decimals; if impressions=0 then 0
      - name: cost_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Cost per conversion equals cost ÷ attributed_conversions_30_d rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_conversions_30_d=0 then 0
      - name: return_on_ad_spend
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: ROAS equals attributed_sales_30_d ÷ cost rounded to 4 decimals; if cost=0 or attributed_sales_30_d=0 then 0
      - name: units_per_conversion
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Units per conversion equals attributed_units_ordered_30_d ÷ attributed_conversions_30_d rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_conversions_30_d=0 then 0
      - name: avg_order_value
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average order value equals attributed_sales_30_d ÷ attributed_units_ordered_30_d rounded to 2 decimals; if attributed_units_ordered_30_d=0 then 0
      - name: _dbt_loaded_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Source ingestion timestamp carried through unchanged

      business_logic: '- Source: intermediate.int_ad_reporting__keyword_report (fields used: platform, account_id, campaign_id, ad_group_id, keyword_id, date_day, match_type, impressions, clicks, interactions, cost, conversions, conversions_value, view_through_conversions, attributed_sales_14_d, attributed_sales_30_d, attributed_conversions_14_d, attributed_conversions_30_d, click_through_rate, cost_per_click, cost_per_thousand_impressions, cost_per_conversion, return_on_ad_spend, _dbt_loaded_at). - Filter: exclude records where platform = ''placeholder''. - Renames/mappings: - date_day -> report_date - cost -> spend - attributed_sales_14_d -> attributed_sales_14_day - attributed_sales_30_d -> attributed_sales_30_day - attributed_conversions_14_d -> attributed_conversions_14_day - attributed_conversions_30_d -> attributed_conversions_30_day - click_through_rate -> ctr_percent - cost_per_click -> cpc - cost_per_thousand_impressions -> cpm - cost_per_conversion -> cpa - return_on_ad_spend -> roas - _dbt_loaded_at -> data_last_updated - Set to NULL (not available from source): campaign_name, ad_group_name, keyword_text, ad_network_type, device_type, quality_score, first_page_cpc, top_of_page_cpc, first_position_cpc, search_impression_share, search_rank_lost_impression_share, search_budget_lost_impression_share, interaction_event_types. - Derive mart_created_at as CURRENT_TIMESTAMP at query execution time. - Ordering applied in the SQL: ORDER BY platform, account_id, campaign_id, ad_group_id, keyword_id, report_date DESC (affects query output order only). - Unless otherwise noted, values are passed through 1:1 from source; NULLs are preserved; no rounding or re-scaling is applied beyond source types.'
